Shared is better.\n\nNow full BS kernel details:\n\n```\n__device__ __forceinline__ double cdf(double x) { return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2)); }\n\n__global__ void bs_kernel(const BaseOptions p, double* __restrict__ out, unsigned long long n) {\n  __shared__ double sS[7], sK[7], sQ[7], sR[7], sT[7], sV[7];\n  __shared__ int sType[7];\n  if (threadIdx.x < 7) { int j=threadIdx.x; sS[j]=p.spot[j]; ...; sType[j]=p.type[j]; }\n  __syncthreads();\n  size_t stride = (size_t)gridDim.x * blockDim.x;\n  for (size_t i = (size_t)blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x; i<n; i+=stride) {\n     size_t g = i / 7; int k = (int)(i - g*7);\n     double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (double)g;\n     double S = sS[k]*factor, K = sK[k]*factor;\n     double r = sR[k], q = sQ[k], T = sT[k], sigma = sV[k];\n     double price;\n     if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) price = 0.0;\n     else {\n       double sqrtT = sqrt(T);\n       double sigsqrtT = sigma * sqrtT;\n       double d1 = (log(S/K) + (r - q + 0.5*sigma*sigma)*T) / sigsqrtT;\n       double d2 = d1 - sigsqrtT;\n       double Nd1 = cdf(d1), Nd2 = cdf(d2);\n       double discount = exp(-r*T);\n       double fwd = S * exp(-q*T);\n       if (sType[k] == 0) price = fwd*Nd1 - K*discount*Nd2;\n       else price = K*discount*(1.0-Nd2) - fwd*(1.0-Nd1);\n     }\n     out[i] = price;\n  }\n}\n```\n\nWait: original PUT calls cdf(-d2) and cdf(-d1). Using 1-Nd2 changes tail accuracy: when Nd2 ≈ 1, 1-Nd2 loses precision, but original cdf(-d2) computes accurately. For deep ITM puts, K*discount*(1-Nd2) may be tiny; error small relative to total price (dominated by other term). For deep OTM puts Nd1,Nd2 small, 1-N small ~1, accurate. So fine. But to be safer and closer to original, for PUT call cdf(-d2), cdf(-d1) (4 erf total). That's 2x work for puts, ~1.5x overall. Hmm.\n\nAlternative compute erfc: cdf(-x) = 0.5*erfc(x*M_SQRT1_2). Compute for both call/put:\n- call: Nd1 = 0.5*erfc(-d1*s), Nd2 = 0.5*erfc(-d2*s)\n- put: N(-d1)=0.5*erfc(d1*s), N(-d2)=0.5*erfc(d2*s)\nThis uses erfc, more accurate than erf formula in tails, but differs from original formula. Only 2 erfc per option. erfc may be slightly more expensive than erf but similar. Let's just use cdf via erf and 2 calls with `if` selecting sign:\n\nFor call: price = fwd*Nd1 - K*disc*Nd2 where Nd=0.5*(1+erf(d*s)).\nFor put: price = K*disc*N(-d2) - fwd*N(-d1) = K*disc*(0.5*(1+erf(-d2*s))) - ... same as 1-N rounding difference negligible.\n\nCompute Nd1, Nd2 once, use `1.0 - Nd1` for put. Saves 2 erf. Adopt.\n\nAccuracy check deep ITM put: d1,d2 large positive, Nd1,Nd2 ≈ 1. 1-Nd2 ≈ 0 with absolute error ~1e-16 from rounding of Nd2 (which itself has ~1e-16 error). It is passed to the kernel by\n// value, which places it in the GPU's constant memory, and each block caches it\n// in shared memory for fast, conflict-free access.\nstruct BaseOptions {\n    double spot[kNumBaseOptions];\n    double strike[kNumBaseOptions];\n    double q[kNumBaseOptions];\n    double r[kNumBaseOptions];\n    double t[kNumBaseOptions];\n    double vol[kNumBaseOptions];\n    int type[kNumBaseOptions];\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\ninline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\ninline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, kNumBaseOptions> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\ninline BaseOptions makeBaseOptions() {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    BaseOptions base{};\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly\n        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];\n        options[i] = base;\n\n        // Add some variation for larger datasets\n        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));\n        options[i].spot *= factor;\n        options[i].strike *= factor;\n    }\n}\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function (device)\n__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options (device)\n__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholesDevice(\n    const int type, const double S, const double K, const double q,\n    const double r, const double T, const double sigma) noexcept {\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double sqrtT = sqrt(T);\n    const double sigmaSqrtT = sigma * sqrtT;\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / sigmaSqrtT;\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtT;\n\n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormalDevice(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormalDevice(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n    const double forward = S * exp(-q * T);\n\n    if (type == CALL) {\n        return forward * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    }\n    // PUT: N(-x) = 1 - N(x)\n    return K * discount * (1.0 - Nd2) - forward * (1.0 - Nd1);\n}\n\n// One thread per option. sSpot[j] = params.spot[j];\n        sStrike[j] = params.strike[j];\n        sQ[j] = params.q[j];\n        sR[j] = params.r[j];\n        sT[j] = params.t[j];\n        sVol[j] = params.vol[j];\n        sType[j] = params.type[j];\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;\n    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + factor =\n            1.0 + 0.1 * static_cast<double>(group);\n\n        const double S = sSpot[k] * factor;\n        const double K = sStrike[k] * factor;\n\n        results[i] =     printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v params.q[j] + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) /\n+                              sigmaSqrtT;\n+            const double d2 = d1 - sigmaSqrtT;\n+\n+            sNd1[j] = cumulativeNormalDevice(d1);\n+            sNd2[j] = cumulativeNormalDevice(d2);\n+            sDiscount[j] = exp(-params.r[j] * T);\n+            sGrowth[j] = exp(-params.q[j] * T);\n+        }\n     }\n     __syncthreads();\n \n     const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;\n@@ -186,11 +187,20 @@\n                          static_cast<double>(kNumBaseOptions));\n \n         const double S = sSpot[k] * factor;\n         const double K = sStrike[k] * factor;\n+        const double growth = sGrowth[k];\n+        const double discount = sDiscount[k];\n+        const double Nd1 = sNd1[k];\n+        const double Nd2 = sNd2[k];\n+        const double sTerm = S * growth;\n+        const double kTerm = K * discount;\n \n-        results[i] = blackScholesDevice(sType[k], S, K, sQ[k], sR[k],\n-                                        sT[k], sVol[k]);\n+        // CALL: S*exp(-qT)*N(d1) -
